Model before rewriting:

letting steps be 7
letting SIZE_A be 8
letting SIZE_B be 5
letting SIZE_C be 3
letting STEPS be domain int(0..steps)
letting STEPS1 be domain int(1..steps)
find bucketA: matrix indexed by [STEPS] of int(0..SIZE_A)
find bucketB: matrix indexed by [STEPS] of int(0..SIZE_B)
find bucketC: matrix indexed by [STEPS] of int(0..SIZE_C)

such that

SIZE_A > SIZE_B,
SIZE_B > SIZE_C,
bucketA[0] = 0,
bucketB[0] = SIZE_B,
bucketC[0] = SIZE_C,
bucketA[steps] = (sum([SIZE_B,SIZE_C;int(1..)]) / 2),
bucketB[steps] = (sum([SIZE_B,SIZE_C;int(1..)]) / 2),
bucketC[steps] <= 1,
and([ sum([sum([bucketC[step],bucketB[step];int(1..)]),bucketA[step];int(1..)]) = sum([SIZE_B,SIZE_C;int(1..)]) | step : STEPS ]),
and([ or([or([or([or([or([and([and([bucketA[(step - 1)] > 0,bucketB[(step - 1)] < SIZE_B;int(1..)]),and([bucketB[step] = min([SIZE_B,sum([bucketA[(step - 1)],bucketB[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..2)]),bucketA[step] = sum([(bucketA[(step - 1)] - bucketB[step]),bucketB[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),and([and([bucketA[(step - 1)] > 0,bucketC[(step - 1)] < SIZE_C;int(1..)]),and([bucketC[step] = min([SIZE_C,sum([bucketA[(step - 1)],bucketC[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..2)]),bucketA[step] = sum([(bucketA[(step - 1)] - bucketC[step]),bucketC[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),and([and([bucketB[(step - 1)] > 0,bucketA[(step - 1)] < SIZE_A;int(1..)]),and([bucketA[step] = min([SIZE_A,sum([bucketA[(step - 1)],bucketB[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..2)]),bucketB[step] = sum([(bucketB[(step - 1)] - bucketA[step]),bucketA[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),and([and([bucketB[(step - 1)] > 0,bucketC[(step - 1)] < SIZE_C;int(1..)]),and([bucketC[step] = min([SIZE_C,sum([bucketB[(step - 1)],bucketC[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..2)]),bucketB[step] = sum([(bucketB[(step - 1)] - bucketC[step]),bucketC[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),and([and([bucketC[(step - 1)] > 0,bucketA[(step - 1)] < SIZE_A;int(1..)]),and([bucketA[step] = min([SIZE_A,sum([bucketA[(step - 1)],bucketC[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..2)]),bucketC[step] = sum([(bucketC[(step - 1)] - bucketA[step]),bucketA[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]),and([and([bucketC[(step - 1)] > 0,bucketB[(step - 1)] < SIZE_B;int(1..)]),and([bucketB[step] = min([SIZE_B,sum([bucketB[(step - 1)],bucketC[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..2)]),bucketC[step] = sum([(bucketC[(step - 1)] - bucketB[step]),bucketB[(step - 1)];int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]) | step : STEPS1 ])
